# Source archive index

This archive is a mechanically normalized copy of the usable journal material. Curated guides live in the numbered topic directories; this area preserves provenance and historical command output. Editor backups (`*~`) and OS/IDE metadata are intentionally excluded. Secrets/credential CSV files are cataloged but not reproduced.
